Internal convolve:
```
def conv_square_fft(a):
    n=a.size
    L=1 << (2*n-2).bit_length()
    fa=np.fft.rfft(a,L)
    b=np.fft.irfft(fa*fa, L)
    b=b[:2*n-1]
    # due to numerical negative small, clip? max maybe positive; for score use max of b, if small negative okay. But if all negative? not.
    return b
```
For conv different:
```
def conv_fft(a,b):
    L=1 << (len(a)+len(b)-2).bit_length()
    fa=rfft(a,L); fb=rfft(b,L); c=irfft(fa*fb,L)[:len(a)+len(b)-1]
```
Use length L optimized: For square L = 1 << ((2*n-2).bit_length())? If 2n-1 =9, bit_length of 8? Example n=4, need length7. 1 << ( (2*n-2).bit_length() )? 2n-2=6 bit_length3 ->8 good. For exact power, if length8 need 8; (7).bit_length()=3 ->8. Good.

Score:
```
def score_fft(a):
    s=a.sum()
    if s<=0: return inf
    a=a/s
    b=conv_square_fft(a)
    return 2*n*np.max(b)
```
Since normalized, average=1/(2n-1). `np.max(b)` maybe negative due to negative small? Sequence nonnegative so positive. Use max.

```
def active_lp_step(f, M, active_rows, U, max_rows, max_solves, time_budget):
    n=f.size
    h=None
    for it in range(max_solves):
       if time...? no within solve; but before
       rows = np.unique(active_rows)
       # sort; if len>max_rows, select top? maybe keep max_rows largest by f current? We can truncate

           # choose rows with largest violation? but we don't have; keep first? Better compute current b? too expensive maybe. Just take top max_rows by current violation? We can compute r for h? Not.
       A = build_conv(rows,n)
       res=linprog(c=-np.ones(n), A_ub=A, b_ub=np.ones(len(rows)), bounds=(0,U), method='highs')
       if res.status not in [0]: return None
       h=res.x
       sumh=h.sum()
       if sumh<=0: return
       # check full violation
       g=M*h
       r=conv_fft(f,g) / M  # since C h
       maxv=r.max()
       if maxv <= 1+1e-7:
           return h/sumh, maxv
       # add violated rows
       viol=r > 1+tol
       if any:
          idx=np.where(viol)[0]
          # add top viol_count e.g. min(500, len(idx)) sorted by r desc
          order=np.argsort(r[idx])[-maxadd:][::-1]
          add=idx[order]
       else: max not? if maxv>1 due numeric, use argmax.
       active_rows = np.unique(np.concatenate([active_rows, add]))

    # if loop ends, return feasible? return h/sumh if best
```

`normalize_nonnegative(a, floor=0.0)`:
```
def normalize_nonnegative(a, min_sum=1e-300):
    a=np.asarray(a,dtype=np.float64)
    a=np.where(np.isfinite(a), a, 0.0)
    a=np.clip(a,0.0,1000.0)
    s=a.sum()
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 1e-15:
        return np.ones_like(a, dtype=np.float64)/a.size
    return a/s
```
If a length arbitrary; if height_sequence_1 None.

`fft_conv_square(a)`:
```
def convolution_square_fft(a):
    n=a.size
    m=2*n-1
    L=1 << (m-1).bit_length()
    fa=np.fft.rfft(a, L)
    fa *= fa
    b=np.fft.irfft(fa, L)
    b=b[:m]
    # Numerical: set tiny negative to 0? For max, maybe not if all positive; but if due >? Use np.maximum(b,0)
    return b
```
`convolution_fft(a,b)`:
```
def convolution_fft(a,b):
    m=a.size+b.size-1
    L=1 << (m-1).bit_length()
    fa=np.fft.rfft(a,L); fb=np.fft.rfft(b,L)
    c=np.fft.irfft(fa*fb,L)
    return c[:m]
```
If b same, use square.
Score:
```
def score_internal(a):
    a=normalize_nonnegative(a)
    n=a.size
    b=conv_square(a)
    mb=b.max()
    if not np.isfinite(mb) or mb<=0: return np.inf
    return float(2.0*n*mb) # since sum=1
```
But if normalized sum exactly 1? normalize returns sum1, small drift. Use `s=a.sum(); score=2*n*mb/(s*s)` for unnormalized maybe. For internal from normalized, fine. If p sum not 1, compute general:
```
def score_unscaled(a):
    s=a.sum(); if s<1e-15 return inf
    b=conv_square(a)
    return 2*n*max(b)/(s*s)
```
This invariance but if we pass normalized, same. For line search candidates, normalize anyway; use fast normalized.

`top_rows(b, K)`: return indices of K largest. `b` length m. If K>=m all.
`gradient_active(p, w)`: w normalized sum1; p_rev=p[::-1]; g=np.convolve? Use FFT or dense? Need compute convolution of length 2n-1? w length m, p_rev length n; p_rev reversed. The linear convolution w * p_rev length m+n-1; we need segment indices n-1 to n-1+n-1? Check earlier formula with p_rev length n, m=2n-1; conv length 3n-3? Wait w length 2n-1, p_rev length n, conv length 3n-3. We need g_i = ∑_{k} w_k p_{k-i}. p_rev index j=n-1-(k-i). We set m = n-1+i? Let's derive carefully.

Let p indices 0..n-1. Want g[i] = ∑_{k=0}^{2n-2} w[k] * p[k-i] if 0≤k-i<n else0.
Let q[j] = p[n-1-j]. We need p[k-i] = q[n-1-(k-i)] = q[n-1-k+i]. Convolution (w*q)[t] = ∑_{k} w[k] q[t-k]. Set t = n-1+i. Then q[t-k] = q[n-1+i-k] = q[n-1-k+i], matches. Good. So g[i] = conv_full(w,q)[n-1+i] for i=0..n-1. Conv length m+n-1 =3n-3. Segment start = n-1, length n. Good.
We can compute with FFT length L next pow2 3n-3. Or use dense np.convolve maybe O(m*n)=40M, too slow? FFT better.
```
def active_gradient(p, w):
    n=p.size
    q=p[::-1]
    L=1 << (3*n-4).bit_length()  # length for 3n-3 is m_exp? max element index 3n-4; bit_length of (3n-4)
    fw=np.fft.rfft(w,L); fq=np.fft.rfft(q,L)
    conv=np.fft.irfft(fw*fq,L)
    g=conv[n-1:n-1+n]
    return 2.0*g
```
Check length: conv length should be len(w)+len(q)-1 = (2n-1)+n-1 =3n-3. Last index 3n-4. Need L >=3n-3. `L=1 << (3*n-3 - 1).bit_length()`? If length =3n-3, next pow2 >= length. We can use `L=1 << (3*n-3).bit_length()`? Example n=4, length=9, bit_length of9=4 ->16, okay. If length power of 8, bit_length of8=4 ->16 too big but fine. Use `1 << (3*n-3).bit_length()`; for length9 gives16. Good. Or overallocate fine.
For conv square, L for length 2n-1: `1 << (2*n-1).bit_length()`. If length=7 bit_length3 ->8. If length=8 bit_length4 ->16; over but okay. Use same for simplicity.

If frac large, underflow. Use `ratio = g / max(gmax,1e-15); q = p * np.exp(-frac*np.clip(ratio, -50, 50))`; normalize. Since g nonnegative? Are w,p nonneg, so g nonnegative. Good.

`local_search` code:
```
def local_search(initial, deadline, rng, verbose=True):
    p=normalize_nonnegative(initial)
    n=p.size
    best=p.copy(); best_score=score_internal(p)
    K_base=max(200, min(2000, int(0.15*(2*n-1))))
    step_fracs=[0.005,0.02,0.05,0.1,0.25,0.5]
    mul_fracs=[0.1,0.3,0.7,1.5,3.0]
    no_improve=0
    it=0
    K=K_base
    while time.monotonic() < deadline-120: # leave time for LP/final? We'll pass deadline with enough.
       b=conv_square(p); M=b.max(); curr_score=2*n*M  # p normalized? ensure sum1; if p sum drift, use score internal maybe.
       # maybe p already best
       if curr_score < best_score: best...
       # active weights
       K = int(K_base * (1.0 + 0.5*np.sin(it/50)))? Avoid too dynamic. Use cycle if no_improve.
       idx=np.argpartition(b, -max(1,K))[-K:] if K < len(b) else np.arange(len(b))
       bw=b[idx]
       # weights: use softmax with tau = mean(b[idx]-b.min())? Or uniform top. Use softmax with small temp to favor highest.
       span = bw.max() - bw.min()
       if span <= 1e-18:
           w_act=np.ones(K)/K
       else:
           # temperature = 0.1*span? But for base span small among top; want more flat for equal, but if some slightly bigger, favor.
           temp = max(1e-15, 0.05*span + 1e-15*bw.max())
           z=(bw - bw.max())/temp
           w_act=np.exp(z); w_act/=w_act.sum()
       w=np.zeros(len(b)); w[idx]=w_act
       g=active_gradient(p,w)
       gmax=max(1e-15, g.max())
       improved=False
       # additive
       # choose base alpha such that alpha*gmax ~ 0.1*pmax
       pmax=max(1e-15,p.max())
       base_alpha = 0.1*pmax/gmax
       for frac in [0.1,0.25,0.5,1.0,2.0,4.0]:
           alpha=base_alpha*frac
           q=p - alpha*g
           q[q<0]=0
           s=q.sum()
           if s<=1e-15: continue
           q/=s
           sc=score_internal(q)
           if sc < best_score - 1e-12:
               best=q.copy(); best_score=sc
           if sc < curr_score - 1e-12:
               p=q; curr_score=sc; improved=True; break
       if not improved:
           base_beta = 1.0
           for beta in [0.1,0.3,0.7,1.5,3.0,6.0]:
               q=p*np.exp(-beta*g/gmax)
               # handle underflow
               q=np.clip(q, 1e-300, None)
               s=q.sum()
               if s<=1e-15: continue
               q/=s
               sc=score_internal(q)
               if sc < best_score - 1e-12:
                   best=q.copy(); best_score=sc
               if sc < curr_score - 1e-12:
                   p=q; curr_score=sc; improved=True; break
       if improved:
          no_improve=0
       else:
          no_improve+=1
          if no_improve == 20:
             # perturb current with best
             noise = rng.random(n)
             p=normalize_nonnegative(0.95*p + 0.05*noise)
             no_improve=0
          elif no_improve == 60:
             p=normalize_nonnegative(0.7*best + 0.3*rng.random(n))
             no_improve=0
          elif no_improve == 120:
             # reset and different K
             p=best.copy()
             K= K//2 if K>200 else K*2
             no_improve=0
       it +=1
       if verbose and it%100==0:
           print(f"local iter {it} current {curr_score:.8f} best {best_score:.8f} time left {deadline-time.monotonic():.1f}")
    return best, best_score
```

Now, active LP matrix builder:
```
def build_conv_matrix(rows, n):
    rows = np.asarray(rows, dtype=np.int64)
    rows = rows[(rows>=0)&(rows<2*n-1)]
    if len(rows)==0: return None
    # sort unique
    rows = np.unique(rows)
    lengths = np.minimum(rows + 1, 2*n - 1 - rows)
    # start array
    start = np.maximum(0, rows - (n-1))
    # lengths end-start+1 = min(rows+1, 2n-1-rows)
    # Build col list
    cols_chunks=[]
    for k in rows:
        st=start[k? no]
    # vectorized: for each k, arange(st, en+1). Could loop.
    indptr = np.empty(len(rows)+1, dtype=np.int64)
    indptr[0]=0
    for idx,k in enumerate(rows):
        st=int(np.maximum(0,k-(n-1)))
        en=int(min(n-1,k))
        cols_chunks.append(np.arange(st, en+1, dtype=np.int32))
        indptr[idx+1]=indptr[idx]+(en-st+1)
    col=np.concatenate(cols_chunks) if cols_chunks else np.empty(0,dtype=np.int32)
    data=np.ones(indptr[-1], dtype=np.float64)
    return csr_matrix((data, col, indptr), shape=(len(rows), n))
```
Loop up to max_rows 3000, fine.
Could optimize by precomputing starts and lengths from arrays but okay.

```
def active_lp_refine(p, deadline, rng, max_outer=5):
    p=normalize_nonnegative(p)
    best=p.copy(); best_score=score_normalized(p,p.size)
    n=p.size
    m=2*n-1
    max_rows = min(m, max(1200, n))  # maybe 4498
    start_rows = min(max_rows, max(400, n//4))
    for outer in range(max_outer):
       if time.monotonic() > deadline - 120: break
       b=conv_square(p); M=b.max()
       if not np.isfinite(M) or M<=0: break
       rows_init=np.argsort(b)[-start_rows:]
       active=np.unique(rows_init)
       improved_outer=False
       for inner in range(8):
           if time.monotonic() > deadline - 90: break
           if len(active)>max_rows:
               # keep rows with largest b among current? or largest previous r? simpler keep top by b now.
               active=np.unique(np.argsort(b)[-max_rows:])
           # Build solve time budget
           remaining=deadline-time.monotonic()
           if remaining < 150: break
           limit = min(300, remaining - 60)
           options = {}
           try:
              # linprog high with time_limit
              A=build_conv_matrix(active,n)
              c=-np.ones(n)
              U=max(1e4, 20.0*p.max()/M)
              if U>1e7: U=1e7
              res=linprog(c, A_ub=A, b_ub=np.ones(len(active)), bounds=(0,U), method='highs', options={'time_limit': int(limit)})
           except Exception as e:
              # try without time limit options? maybe
              try:
                 res=linprog(c, A_ub=A, b_ub=np.ones(len(active)), bounds=(0,U), method='highs')
              except Exception:
                 return best,best_score
           if res.status not in (0,):
              # if time limit status 9 with x? HiGHS may provide pickbest? Not sure. If status 9 (limit) maybe res.x available? We can try if finite.
              if not hasattr(res,'x') or res.x is None or not np.all(np.isfinite(res.x)):
                 break
           h=np.asarray(res.x,dtype=np.float64)
           h=np.clip(h,0,None)
           sumh=h.sum()
           if sumh <= 1e-12: break
           r=convolution_fft(p,h)
           maxr=r.max()
           # Since constraints may be not exact due numerical, tolerance.
           if maxr > 1.0 + 1e-6:
               # add top rows by r
               add_count = min(1000, max(100, max_rows - len(np.unique(active)) if len(active)<max_rows else 300))
               top=np.argsort(r)[-add_count:]
               active=np.unique(np.concatenate([active, top]))
               continue
           cand=normalize_nonnegative(h)
           # line search mix
           cur_score=best_score? Actually use p score current_score=2*n*M.
           cur_score=2*n*M
           if cur_score < best_score: best=p.copy(); best_score=cur_score
           best_q=p.copy(); best_sc=cur_score
           for lam in [0.02,0.05,0.1,0.2,0.4,1.0]:
               q=(1-lam)*p + lam*cand
               q=normalize_nonnegative(q)
               sc=score_normalized(q,n)
               if sc < best_sc:
                   best_sc=sc; best_q=q
           if best_sc < cur_score - 1e-12:
               p=best_q
               if best_sc < best_score:
                   best_score=best_sc; best=p.copy()
               improved_outer=True
               break
           else:
               break
       if not improved_outer:
           break
       # maybe print
    return best,best_score
```

```
def generate_candidates(n, rng):
  cands=[]
  for beta in [0.0,0.2,0.3,0.4,0.45,0.5,0.55,0.6]:
     i=np.arange(n)
     cands.append(1.0/np.sqrt(i+0.75) ** beta? Actually i^-beta)
  # specifically a=1/(i+0.75)**beta
  # sym:
  L=1/np.sqrt(i+0.75)
  R=1/np.sqrt(n-1-i+0.75)
  for gamma in [0,0.25,0.5,1,2,4]:
     cands.append(L + gamma*R)
  # endpoint with finite:
  for alpha in [0.5,1,2,5]:
     cands.append(1.0/(np.sqrt(i+1.0)+alpha*np.sqrt(n-1-i+1.0)))
  # U:
  x=i/(n-1)
  for q in [1,2,4]:
     for alpha in [0.5,1,2,5]:
         cands.append(1.0 + alpha*np.abs(x-0.5)**q)
  # random: 3 uniform, 3 normal positive, 3 smoothed box
  # Evaluate all
```

```
def smooth_random(n,rng,wnd):
  x=(rng.random(n)>0.5).astype(float)
  k=np.ones(max(1,wnd)); x=np.convolve(x,k,mode='same'); return x.clip(0,None)
```

Evaluate candidates:
```
def evaluate_candidates(cands):
 best=None; best_sc=inf
 for cand in cands:
   p=normalize_nonnegative(cand)
   sc=score_normalized(p,n)
   if sc<best_sc and finite: best=p
 return
```

```
def normalize_fast(a):
```

```
import numpy as np
import time

try:
    from scipy.optimize import linprog
    from scipy.sparse import csr_matrix
    _HAS_SCIPY = True
except Exception:
    _HAS_SCIPY = False

def ... helpers ...

def propose_candidate(seed=42, budget_s=1800, **kwargs):
    """Docstring."""
    start=time.monotonic(); deadline=start+max(60.0,float(budget_s)-20.0)
    rng=np.random.default_rng(seed)
    n=choose_length()
    pool=[]
    best,best_score=initial_candidates(n,rng,deadline, pool)
    # local search phases
    if time.monotonic() < deadline-900:
        local_deadline = min(deadline-900, start+700)
        best,best_score=local_search(best, local_deadline, rng, n, pool)
    # LP
    if _HAS_SCIPY and time.monotonic() < deadline-400 and best_score > 1.49:
       best,best_score=active_lp_refine(best, deadline-250, rng, n, pool)
    # final local
    if time.monotonic() < deadline-50:
       best,best_score=local_search(best, deadline-30, rng, n, pool)
    # final exact select
    seq=final_select(pool, n, deadline)
    print("final internal", best_score)
    return seq
```

```
def score_fast(a, n):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
    s = float(a.sum())
    if s <= 1e-15: return np.inf
    a = a / s
    b = convolution_square_fft(a)
    mb = float(np.max(b))
    if not np.isfinite(mb) or mb <= 0: return np.inf
    return 2.0 * n * mb
```

```
def as_length(a,n):
   a=np.asarray(a,dtype=np.float64)
   if a.size==n: return a
   if a.size<=1: return np.full(n, a[0] if size else 1)
   old=np.linspace(0,1,a.size)
   new=np.linspace(0,1,n)
   return np.interp(new, old, a)
```

```
def choose_length():
    seq = globals().get('height_sequence_1', None)
    try:
        sz = len(seq)
    except Exception:
        sz = 0
    if sz >= 1000 and sz <= 5000:
        return int(sz)
    return 4498
```

`global_candidate(n)`:
```
def global_candidate(n):
    seq=globals().get('height_sequence_1', None)
    if seq is None: return None
    try:
       a=np.asarray(seq, dtype=np.float64)
       a=np.ravel(a)
       if a.size==0: return None
       a=np.where(np.isfinite(a), a, 0.0)
       a=np.clip(a,0,1000)
       s=a.sum()
       if s<=0: return None
       if a.size != n:
           old=np.linspace(0,1,a.size)
           new=np.linspace(0,1,n)
           a=np.interp(new, old, a)
       a=a/s
       if np.all(a>=0): return a
    except Exception:
       pass
    return None
```
If a has Nones? np.asarray maybe object; use `astype`? Already dtype float may fail. Good.

`initial_candidates`:
```
def initial_candidates(n, rng, deadline, pool):
   cands=make_candidate_list(n,rng)
   best=None; best_sc=np.inf
   for cand in cands:
       if time.monotonic() > deadline: break
       p=normalize_nonnegative(cand, n) # robust
       sc=score_fast(p,n)
       if np.isfinite(sc):
           add_pool(pool,p,sc)
           if sc<best_sc:
               best=p; best_sc=sc
   if best is None:
       best=normalize_nonnegative(np.ones(n), n); best_sc=2.0
   return best,best_sc
```
`normalize_nonnegative(cand,n)` can take array; if length differs? make list length n; robust.

`normalize_nonnegative` top-level:
```
def normalize_nonnegative(a, expected_n=None):
   a=np.asarray(a,dtype=np.float64).ravel()
   if a.size==0:
       a=np.ones(1)
   a=np.where(np.isfinite(a), a, 0.0)
   a=np.clip(a,0.0,1000.0)
   s=a.sum()
   if not np.isfinite(s) or s<=1e-15:
       return np.ones(a.size if expected_n is None else expected_n) / (a.size if expected_n is None else expected_n)
   return a/s
```
If expected_n passed and a.size != expected_n? We can resample? But functions only use correct length. Use no expected_n? Simpler: no expected_n. For initial cands all n. If global size different, global_candidate resampled. So `normalize_nonnegative(a)`.

`normalize_fast(a)`:
```
def normalize_fast(a):
    a=np.maximum(a,0.0)
    s=a.sum()
    if s<=1e-15: return np.ones_like(a)/a.size
    return a/s
```

Potential issue: `np.maximum` on array with NaN returns NaN? It propagates? `np.maximum(np.nan,0)=nan`. But values finite. For robust, use `np.where(np.isfinite(a), a, 0)` in nonnegative.

Now, `local_search` code length. Need ensure variables defined. Let's write:
```
def local_search(p, deadline, rng, n, pool=None):
    p=normalize_nonnegative(p)
    best=p.copy()
    best_score=score_fast(p,n)
    add_pool(pool,best,best_score)
    m=2*n-1
    no_improve=0
    it=0
    maxK=max(200, int(0.8*m))
    while time.monotonic() < deadline - 15.0:
        b=convolution_square_fft(p)
        M=float(np.max(b)); s=float(p.sum()); curr=2.0*n*M/(s*s)
        if np.isfinite(curr) and curr<best_score:
              best=p.copy(); best_score=curr; add_pool(...)
        # active
        med=float(np.median(b))
        gap=M-med if M>med else 1e-15
        if gap <= 1e-15:
            K = max(1, int(0.5*m))
            idx = np.argpartition(b, -K)[-K:]
        else:
            thresh = M - max(1e-15, 0.1*gap)
            idx = np.where(b >= thresh)[0]
            if idx.size==0: idx=np.array([int(np.argmax(b))])
            if idx.size > maxK:
                idx = np.argpartition(b, -maxK)[-maxK:]
        K=idx.size
        bw=b[idx]
        if K == 1:
            w_idx=np.ones(1)
        else:
            span=float(bw.max()-bw.min())
            if span <= 1e-18:
                w_idx=np.ones(K)/K
            else:
                temp=max(1e-15, 0.05*span)
                z=(bw-bw.max())/temp
                w_idx=np.exp(z)
                w_sum=w_idx.sum()
                if w_sum<=0 or not finite: uniform else w_idx/=w_sum
        w=np.zeros(m); w[idx]=w_idx
        g=active_gradient(p,w)
        gc=g-g.mean()
        gc_max=float(np.max(np.abs(gc)))
        improved=False
        if gc_max > 1e-18:
            pmax=float(np.max(p)); base_alpha=0.25*pmax/gc_max
            for frac in (0.01,0.03,0.1,0.3,0.6,1.0,2.0):
                alpha=base_alpha*frac
                q=p-alpha*gc
                q=np.maximum(q,0.0)
                sq=q.sum()
                if sq <=1e-15: continue
                q=q/sq
                sc=score_fast(q,n)
                if np.isfinite(sc):
                    if sc<best_score-1e-14:
                       best=q.copy(); best_score=sc; add_pool(...)
                    if sc<curr-1e-12:
                       p=q; curr=sc; improved=True; break
        # multiplicative
        if not improved:
            gmax=float(np.max(g))
            if gmax > 1e-18:
              for beta in (0.05,0.1,0.2,0.5,1.0,2.0):
                 ratio=np.clip(g/gmax, 0.0, 50.0)
                 q=p*np.exp(-beta*ratio)
                 q=np.where(q>1e-300, q, 0.0)
                 sq=q.sum()
                 if sq<=1e-15: continue
                 q=q/sq
                 sc=score_fast(q,n)
                 if np.isfinite(sc):
                    ...
        if improved: no_improve=0
        else:
          no_improve +=1
          if no_improve == 30:
             p=normalize_nonnegative(0.95*best + 0.05*rng.random(n))
             no_improve=0
          elif no_improve == 80:
             p=normalize_nonnegative(0.85*best + 0.15*rng.random(n))
             no_improve=0
          elif no_improve == 160:
             p=best.copy()
             no_improve=0
          elif no_improve >= 300:
             p=normalize_nonnegative(0.5*best + 0.5*rng.random(n))
             no_improve=0
        it+=1
        if verbose and it%200 ==0: print(...)
    return best,best_score
```

```
def active_lp_refine(p, deadline, rng, n, pool=None, verbose=False):
    p=normalize_nonnegative(p); best=p.copy(); best_score=score_fast(p,n); add_pool...
    if not _HAS_SCIPY: return best,best_score
    m=2*n-1
    max_rows = min(m, max(1500, int(0.75*m)))? For n4498, 6746 maybe nnz full? 0.75*8995=6746, nnz maybe full 20M? Too much. Choose max_rows = min(m, 3000). If m<3000 use m. For n=4498, 3000.
    start_top = min(800, max(100, n//5))
    strat_count = min(600, m)
    for outer in range(8):
       remaining=deadline-time.monotonic()
       if remaining < 180: break
       b=convolution_square_fft(p); M=float(np.max(b)); s=p.sum(); cur=2nM/s²
       if not finite ... break
       if cur<best_score: update
       strat=np.linspace(0,m-1,strat_count, dtype=int)
       top=np.argsort(b)[-start_top:]
       active=np.unique(np.concatenate([strat, top]))
       improved_any=False
       r=np.zeros(m)
       for inner in range(8):
          remaining=deadline-time.monotonic()
          if remaining < 90: break
          active=np.unique(active)
          if len(active)>max_rows:
             if r.size == m and np.any(r>0): active=np.unique(np.argsort(r)[-max_rows:])
             else: active=np.unique(np.argsort(b)[-max_rows:])
          A=build_conv_matrix(active,n)
          if A is None: break
          U=max(1e4, 50.0*p.max()/M)
          if not finite or U<1: U=1e4
          if U>1e7: U=1e7
          time_limit=int(min(240, remaining-60))
          if time_limit < 30: break
          solved=False
          try:
             res=linprog(np.ones(n, dtype=float)*-1.0, A_ub=A, b_ub=np.ones(len(active)), bounds=(0.0,float(U)), method='highs', options={'time_limit': time_limit})
             solved=True
          except Exception:
             try:
                res=linprog(... no options)
                solved=True
             except Exception:
                break
          if not solved: break
          x=getattr(res,'x',None)
          if x is None: break
          h=np.asarray(x,dtype=np.float64).ravel()
          h=np.where(np.isfinite(h), h, 0.0)
          h=np.maximum(h,0.0)
          sumh=float(h.sum())
          if sumh<=1e-12: break
          r=convolution_fft(p,h)
          maxr=float(np.max(r))
          if maxr > 1.0 + 1e-7:
             # add violated rows
             viol_top = np.argsort(r)[-min(1000, m):]
             if len(active) < max_rows:
                active=np.unique(np.concatenate([active, viol_top]))
             else:
                # blend: keep top max_rows by r, but also ensure some stratified? just top
                active=np.unique(viol_top[-max_rows:])
             continue
          cand=normalize_nonnegative(h)
          best_q=p.copy(); best_sc=cur
          for lam in (0.01,0.02,0.05,0.1,0.2,0.5,1.0):
             q=(1.0-lam)*p + lam*cand
             q=normalize_nonnegative(q)
             sc=score_fast(q,n)
             if np.isfinite(sc) and sc<best_sc-1e-14:
                best_sc=sc; best_q=q.copy()
          if best_sc < cur - 1e-12:
             p=best_q
             if best_sc<best_score:
                best=p.copy(); best_score=best_sc; add_pool(pool,best,best_score)
             improved_any=True
             if verbose: print("LP outer improve", best_sc)
             break
          else:
             break
       if not improved_any:
          break
    return best,best_score
```

```
def final_select(pool, n, internal_best, deadline):
    # pick up to 10 candidates with low internal score and current best
    candidates=[]
    if internal_best is not None: candidates.append(internal_best)
    # manual choose lowest internal from pool
    used=[]
    for _ in range(12):
       best_idx=-1; best_sc=np.inf
       for idx, entry in enumerate(pool):
           if idx in used: continue
           if entry[0] < best_sc:
              best_sc=entry[0]; best_idx=idx
       if best_idx<0: break
       used.append(best_idx)
       candidates.append(pool[best_idx][1])
    ev = globals().get('evaluate_sequence')
    best_seq=np.asarray(internal_best,dtype=np.float64) if internal_best is not None else np.ones(n)
    best_exact=np.inf
    for p in candidates:
       seq=[float(v) for v in np.nan_to_num(np.clip(np.asarray(p,dtype=np.float64),0.0,1000.0))]
       if callable(ev):
           try: exact=ev(seq)
           except Exception: exact=np.inf
       else: exact=np.inf
       if np.isfinite(exact):
          best_exact=exact; best_seq=np.asarray(seq)
       elif best_seq is None: ...
       if time.monotonic() > deadline - 5: break
    return [float(v) for v in best_seq]
```

```
def clean_sequence(p,n):
    arr=np.asarray(p,dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    if arr.size != n:
       if arr.size>0: resample; else ones
    arr=np.where(np.isfinite(arr), arr, 0.0)
    arr=np.clip(arr,0,1000)
    s=arr.sum()
    if s <=0: arr=np.ones(n)
    return arr
```

```
def propose_candidate(seed=42, budget_s=1800, **kwargs):
    """..."""
    start=time.monotonic()
    total=max(60.0, float(budget_s))
    deadline=start+max(0.0,total-30.0)
    rng=np.random.default_rng(seed)
    n=choose_length()
    pool=[]
    print(f"starting n={n} budget={total}")
    cand_deadline=min(deadline-800.0, start+120.0)
    if cand_deadline < start+10: cand_deadline=start+10
    best,best_score=initial_candidates(n,rng,cand_deadline,pool)
    print("initial best", best_score)
    # first local
    if time.monotonic() < deadline - 900.0:
       ld=min(deadline-900.0, start+700.0)
       best,best_score=local_search(best, ld, rng, n, pool, verbose=True)
       print("local1", best_score)
    # LP
    if _HAS_SCIPY and time.monotonic() < deadline - 400.0 and best_score > 1.48:
       ld=min(deadline-250.0, start+1500.0) # maybe
       best,best_score=active_lp_refine(best, ld, rng, n, pool, verbose=True)
       print("after LP", best_score)
    # second local
    if time.monotonic() < deadline - 40.0:
       best,best_score=local_search(best, deadline-30.0, rng, n, pool, verbose=False)
       print("after local2", best_score)
    seq=final_select(pool,n,best,deadline)
    print("returned")
    return seq
```

```
def select_final_candidates(pool, count):
    if pool is None: return []
    available=list(range(len(pool)))
    chosen=[]
    for _ in range(count):
       if not available: break
       best_idx=available[0]
       best_sc=pool[best_idx][0]
       for idx in available[1:]:
          sc=pool[idx][0]
          if sc < best_sc:
             best_sc=sc; best_idx=idx
       chosen.append(pool[best_idx][1])
       available.remove(best_idx)
    return chosen
```
No lambda. Use `available.remove` O(n) small.

If pool contains duplicates, evaluating duplicates okay.

Now, ensure `evaluate_sequence` may not be callable if not in globals due sandbox. Then best_exact remains inf; final uses internal best.

Potential issue: Our internal best `p` normalized sum1; final sequence sum1. Evaluator protects sum too close zero? sum=1 okay. If list all floats. Good.

The line search will reject if score bad. It might rarely improve. Multiplicative may be safer. We can try multiplicative first maybe? For base, multiplicative reduces left only, no mass to right; additive centered helps. Keep both. The line search tries additive; if no improvement, multiplicative. But if additive candidates all bad due clipping, multiplicative may improve. Good.

Potential negative gc: In multiplicative, use g nonnegative; if g zeros, ratio0; unchanged. Good.

If b has outlier high, med lower, threshold M-0.1gap may include many if gap large? For sequence with score high and one huge peak, gap large; threshold low, active many, gradient broad. Okay.

```python
"""
Search for a nonnegative sequence minimizing

    2 * n * max_k((a*a)_k) / (sum a)^2

with an FFT-based local search, a cutting-plane linear-programming
refinement inspired by the previous SOTA, and multiple parametric/random
restarts.  The objective is scale invariant, so all internal searches use
sequences normalized to sum one.
"""

import time
import numpy as np

try:
    from scipy.optimize import linprog
    from scipy.sparse import csr_matrix
    _HAS_SCIPY = True
except Exception:
    _HAS_SCIPY = False


def choose_length():
    """Pick a safe search length, preferably the supplied construction length."""
    seq = globals().get("height_sequence_1", None)
    try:
        sz = int(len(seq))
    except Exception:
        sz = 0
    if 800 <= sz <= 5000:
        return sz
    return 4498


def clean_sequence(a, n):
    """Return a finite nonnegative length-n array."""
    arr = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    if arr.size == 0:
        arr = np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64)
    elif arr.size != n:
        if arr.size == 1:
            arr = np.full(n, float(arr[0]), dtype=np.float64)
        else:
            old = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, arr.size)
            new = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n)
            arr = np.interp(new, old, arr)
    arr = np.where(np.isfinite(arr), arr, 0.0)
    arr = np.clip(arr, 0.0, 1000.0)
    s = float(arr.sum())
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 1e-300:
        arr = np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64)
    else:
        arr = arr / s
    return arr


def normalize_fast(a):
    """Fast normalization assuming finite nonnegative input."""
    a = np.maximum(a, 0.0)
    s = float(a.sum())
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 1e-300:
        return np.ones_like(a, dtype=np.float64) / max(1, a.size)
    return a / s


def convolution_square_fft(a):
    """FFT square convolution of a real sequence with itself."""
    n = a.size
    m = 2 * n - 1
    L = 1 << (m).bit_length()
    fa = np.fft.rfft(a, L)
    fa = fa * fa
    c = np.fft.irfft(fa, L)
    c = c[:m]
    # Tiny negative values can appear from roundoff; they are irrelevant for max.
    return c


def convolution_fft(a, b):
    """FFT linear convolution of two real sequences."""
    m = a.size + b.size - 1
    L = 1 << (m).bit_length()
    fa = np.fft.rfft(a, L)
    fb = np.fft.rfft(b, L)
    c = np.fft.irfft(fa * fb, L)
    return c[:m]


def score_fast(a, n):
    """Fast scale-invariant score using FFT."""
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    s = float(a.sum())
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 1e-300:
        return float("inf")
    a = a / s
    b = convolution_square_fft(a)
    mb = float(np.max(b))
    if not np.isfinite(mb) or mb <= 0.0:
        return float("inf")
    return float(2.0 * n * mb)


def active_gradient(p, w):
    """
    Gradient of sum_k w_k (p*p)_k with respect to p, for normalized w.

    Returns vector g with g_i = 2 * sum_k w_k p_{k-i}, using valid indices.
    """
    n = p.size
    m = 2 * n - 1
    q = p[::-1]
    conv_len = m + n - 1
    L = 1 << (conv_len).bit_length()
    fw = np.fft.rfft(w, L)
    fq = np.fft.rfft(q, L)
    cc = np.fft.irfft(fw * fq, L)
    g = cc[n - 1:n - 1 + n]
    return 2.0 * g


def add_pool(pool, p, score):
    """Keep a small configurable pool of low internal-score sequences."""
    if pool is None:
        return
    if not np.isfinite(score):
        return
    if len(pool) < 64:
        pool.append((float(score), np.asarray(p, dtype=np.float64).copy()))
    else:
        worst_idx = 0
        worst_sc = float("inf")
        for i, entry in enumerate(pool):
            if entry[0] > worst_sc:
                worst_sc = entry[0]
                worst_idx = i
        if float(score) < worst_sc:
            pool[worst_idx] = (float(score), np.asarray(p, dtype=np.float64).copy())


def global_candidate(n):
    """Use the previously found construction if available, resampled to n."""
    seq = globals().get("height_sequence_1", None)
    if seq is None:
        return None
    try:
        a = np.asarray(seq, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
        if a.size == 0:
            return None
        a = np.where(np.isfinite(a), a, 0.0)
        a = np.clip(a, 0.0, 1000.0)
        s = float(a.sum())
        if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 1e-300:
            return None
        if a.size != n:
            old = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, a.size)
            new = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n)
            a = np.interp(new, old, a)
        a = a / s
        return a
    except Exception:
        return None


def smooth_random_binary(n, rng, period):
    """A random binary signal smoothed by a box filter."""
    x = (rng.random(n) < 0.5).astype(np.float64)
    w = max(1, int(period))
    x = np.convolve(x, np.ones(w, dtype=np.float64), mode="same")
    x = np.maximum(x, 0.0)
    return x


def make_candidate_list(n, rng):
    """Generate diverse deterministic and random initial candidates."""
    i = np.arange(n, dtype=np.float64)
    x = i / float(max(1, n - 1))
    eps = 0.75
    left = 1.0 / np.sqrt(i + eps)
    right = 1.0 / np.sqrt((n - 1 - i) + eps)
    cands = []

    g = global_candidate(n)
    if g is not None:
        cands.append(g)
        cands.append(normalize_fast(0.9 * g + 0.1 * rng.random(n)))
        cands.append(normalize_fast(0.7 * g + 0.3 * rng.random(n)))

    cands.append(np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64))

    for beta in (0.2, 0.3, 0.4, 0.45, 0.5, 0.55, 0.6, 0.7):
        cands.append((i + eps) ** (-beta))
        cands.append(((n - 1 - i) + eps) ** (-beta))

    mixes = [
        (1.0, 0.0),
        (0.9, 0.03),
        (0.8, 0.08),
        (0.7, 0.17),
        (0.6, 0.35),
        (0.5, 0.65),
        (0.4, 1.2),
        (0.3, 2.0),
    ]
    for a, b in mixes:
        cands.append(a * left + b * right)

    for denom in (left + right, 0.5 * left + right, left + 0.5 * right):
        cands.append(1.0 / np.maximum(denom, 1e-12))

    for alpha in (0.5, 1.0, 2.0, 4.0):
        for q in (1.0, 2.0, 4.0):
            cands.append(1.0 + alpha * np.abs(x - 0.5) ** q)

    for alpha in (0.2, 0.5, 1.0, 2.0):
        cands.append(np.exp(-alpha * x) + np.exp(-alpha * (1.0 - x)))

    for _ in range(8):
        cands.append(1e-6 + rng.random(n))

    for _ in range(4):
        cands.append(np.clip(rng.normal(1.0, 0.5, n), 0.1, None))

    for w in (n // 50, n // 20, n // 10, n // 4):
        cands.append(smooth_random_binary(n, rng, w))

    return cands


def initial_candidates(n, rng, deadline, pool):
    """Evaluate a set of simple candidates and return the best one."""
    cands = make_candidate_list(n, rng)
    best = None
    best_score = float("inf")
    for cand in cands:
        if time.monotonic() > deadline:
            break
        p = clean_sequence(cand, n)
        sc = score_fast(p, n)
        add_pool(pool, p, sc)
        if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_score:
            best = p
            best_score = sc
    if best is None:
        best = clean_sequence(np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64), n)
        best_score = score_fast(best, n)
    return best, best_score


def local_search(p, deadline, rng, n, pool=None, verbose=False):
    """
    FFT subgradient / centered-direction local search.

    Uses the current near-maximal convolution lags to form a weighted
    subgradient, then tries short step lengths.  The best sequence is
    preserved and occasionally a perturbation is used to escape plateaus.
    """
    p = clean_sequence(p, n)
    best = p.copy()
    best_score = score_fast(p, n)
    add_pool(pool, best, best_score)

    m = 2 * n - 1
    max_k = max(200, int(0.85 * m))
    no_improve = 0
    it = 0

    while time.monotonic() < deadline - 15.0:
        b = convolution_square_fft(p)
        s = float(p.sum())
        M = float(np.max(b))
        if not np.isfinite(M) or M <= 0.0 or not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 0.0:
            break
        curr = float(2.0 * n * M / (s * s))

        if np.isfinite(curr) and curr < best_score - 1e-14:
            best = p.copy()
            best_score = curr
            add_pool(pool, best, best_score)

        med = float(np.median(b))
        gap = M - med if M > med else 1e-15

        if gap <= 1e-15:
            k = max(1, int(0.5 * m))
            idx = np.argpartition(b, -k)[-k:]
        else:
            thresh = M - max(1e-15, 0.10 * gap)
            idx = np.where(b >= thresh)[0]
            if idx.size == 0:
                idx = np.array([int(np.argmax(b))], dtype=np.int64)
            if idx.size > max_k:
                idx = np.argpartition(b, -max_k)[-max_k:]

        bw = b[idx]
        K = int(idx.size)
        if K <= 0:
            idx = np.array([int(np.argmax(b))], dtype=np.int64)
            bw = b[idx]
            K = 1

        if K == 1 or (bw.max() - bw.min()) <= 1e-18:
            w_idx = np.ones(K, dtype=np.float64) / float(K)
        else:
            temp = max(1e-15, 0.05 * float(bw.max() - bw.min()))
            z = (bw - bw.max()) / temp
            w_idx = np.exp(z)
            wsz = float(w_idx.sum())
            if not np.isfinite(wsz) or wsz <= 0.0:
                w_idx = np.ones(K, dtype=np.float64) / float(K)
            else:
                w_idx = w_idx / wsz

        w = np.zeros(m, dtype=np.float64)
        w[idx] = w_idx
        g = active_gradient(p, w)
        gc = g - float(np.mean(g))
        gc_max = float(np.max(np.abs(gc)))

        improved = False

        if gc_max > 1e-18:
            pmax = float(np.max(p))
            base_alpha = 0.25 * pmax / gc_max
            for frac in (0.02, 0.05, 0.10, 0.20, 0.40, 0.80, 1.60):
                alpha = base_alpha * frac
                q = p - alpha * gc
                q = np.maximum(q, 0.0)
                qs = float(q.sum())
                if not np.isfinite(qs) or qs <= 1e-300:
                    continue
                q = q / qs
                sc = score_fast(q, n)
                if not np.isfinite(sc):
                    continue
                if sc < best_score - 1e-14:
                    best = q.copy()
                    best_score = sc
                    add_pool(pool, best, best_score)
                if sc < curr - 1e-12:
                    p = q
                    curr = sc
                    improved = True
                    break

        if not improved:
            gmax = float(np.max(g))
            if gmax > 1e-18:
                for beta in (0.05, 0.10, 0.20, 0.50, 1.00, 2.00):
                    ratio = np.clip(g / gmax, 0.0, 40.0)
                    q = p * np.exp(-beta * ratio)
                    q = np.where(q > 1e-300, q, 0.0)
                    qs = float(q.sum())
                    if not np.isfinite(qs) or qs <= 1e-300:
                        continue
                    q = q / qs
                    sc = score_fast(q, n)
                    if not np.isfinite(sc):
                        continue
                    if sc < best_score - 1e-14:
                        best = q.copy()
                        best_score = sc
                        add_pool(pool, best, best_score)
                    if sc < curr - 1e-12:
                        p = q
                        curr = sc
                        improved = True
                        break

        if improved:
            no_improve = 0
        else:
            no_improve += 1
            if no_improve == 30:
                p = normalize_fast(0.95 * best + 0.05 * rng.random(n))
                no_improve = 0
            elif no_improve == 90:
                p = normalize_fast(0.85 * best + 0.15 * rng.random(n))
                no_improve = 0
            elif no_improve == 180:
                p = best.copy()
                no_improve = 0
            elif no_improve >= 320:
                p = normalize_fast(0.5 * best + 0.5 * rng.random(n))
                no_improve = 0

        it += 1
        if verbose and it % 250 == 0:
            print(
                "local iteration", it,
                "current", f"{curr:.10f}",
                "best", f"{best_score:.10f}",
                "left", f"{max(0.0, deadline-time.monotonic()):.1f}s",
                flush=True
            )

    return best, best_score


def build_conv_matrix(rows, n):
    """Build sparse matrix C where (C y)_k = sum_i y_{k-i} for selected k."""
    rows = np.asarray(rows, dtype=np.int64).ravel()
    rows = rows[(rows >= 0) & (rows < (2 * n - 1))]
    if rows.size == 0:
        return None
    rows = np.unique(rows)

    chunks = []
    indptr = np.empty(rows.size + 1, dtype=np.int64)
    indptr[0] = 0
    nm1 = n - 1
    for idx, k in enumerate(rows):
        st = max(0, int(k) - nm1)
        en = min(n - 1, int(k))
        chunks.append(np.arange(st, en + 1, dtype=np.int32))
        indptr[idx + 1] = indptr[idx] + (en - st + 1)

    if len(chunks) == 0:
        return None

    col = np.concatenate(chunks)
    data = np.ones(int(indptr[-1]), dtype=np.float64)
    return csr_matrix((data, col, indptr), shape=(rows.size, n))


def active_lp_refine(p, deadline, rng, n, pool=None, verbose=False):
    """
    Cutting-plane LP refinement.

    For the current normalized f, solve approximately

        max sum(y)   s.t. (f*y)_k <= 1, y >= 0,

    adding violated convolution constraints.  The LP variable y is
    proportional to the SOTA g / M.  Mixing y / sum(y) with f is attempted.
    """
    p = clean_sequence(p, n)
    best = p.copy()
    best_score = score_fast(p, n)
    add_pool(pool, best, best_score)

    if not _HAS_SCIPY:
        return best, best_score

    m = 2 * n - 1
    max_rows = min(m, 3000)
    start_top = min(800, max(200, n // 5))
    strat_count = min(600, m)
    c = -np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64)

    for outer in range(8):
        remaining = deadline - time.monotonic()
        if remaining < 180.0:
            break

        b = convolution_square_fft(p)
        s = float(p.sum())
        M = float(np.max(b))
        if not np.isfinite(M) or M <= 0.0:
            break
        cur_score = float(2.0 * n * M / (s * s))

        if np.isfinite(cur_score) and cur_score < best_score - 1e-14:
            best = p.copy()
            best_score = cur_score
            add_pool(pool, best, best_score)

        strat = np.linspace(0, m - 1, strat_count, dtype=np.int64)
        top = np.argsort(b)[-start_top:]
        active = np.unique(np.concatenate((strat, top)))
        r = np.zeros(m, dtype=np.float64)
        improved_any = False

        for inner in range(10):
            remaining = deadline - time.monotonic()
            if remaining < 90.0:
                break

            active = np.unique(active)
            if active.size > max_rows:
                if r.size == m and np.any(r > 0.0):
                    active = np.unique(np.argsort(r)[-max_rows:])
                else:
                    active = np.unique(np.argsort(b)[-max_rows:])

            A = build_conv_matrix(active, n)
            if A is None:
                break

            pmax = float(np.max(p))
            U = max(1.0e4, 50.0 * pmax / M)
            if not np.isfinite(U) or U < 1.0:
                U = 1.0e4
            U = min(U, 1.0e7)

            time_limit = int(min(240.0, remaining - 60.0))
            if time_limit < 30:
                break

            solved = False
            res = None
            try:
                res = linprog(
                    c,
                    A_ub=A,
                    b_ub=np.ones(active.size, dtype=np.float64),
                    bounds=(0.0, float(U)),
                    method="highs",
                    options={"time_limit": time_limit},
                )
                solved = True
            except Exception:
                try:
                    res = linprog(
                        c,
                        A_ub=A,
                        b_ub=np.ones(active.size, dtype=np.float64),
                        bounds=(0.0, float(U)),
                        method="highs",
                    )
                    solved = True
                except Exception:
                    solved = False

            if not solved:
                break

            x = getattr(res, "x", None)
            if x is None:
                break
            h = np.asarray(x, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
            h = np.where(np.isfinite(h), h, 0.0)
            h = np.maximum(h, 0.0)
            sumh = float(h.sum())
            if not np.isfinite(sumh) or sumh <= 1e-12:
                break

            r = convolution_fft(p, h)
            maxr = float(np.max(r))

            if maxr > 1.0 + 1e-7:
                top_viol = np.argsort(r)[-min(1000, m):]
                if active.size < max_rows:
                    active = np.unique(np.concatenate((active, top_viol)))
                else:
                    active = np.unique(top_viol[-max_rows:])
                continue

            cand = normalize_fast(h)
            best_q = p.copy()
            best_sc = cur_score

            for lam in (0.01, 0.02, 0.05, 0.10, 0.20, 0.50, 1.0):
                q = (1.0 - lam) * p + lam * cand
                q = clean_sequence(q, n)
                sc = score_fast(q, n)
                if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_sc - 1e-14:
                    best_sc = sc
                    best_q = q.copy()

            if best_sc < cur_score - 1e-12:
                p = best_q
                if best_sc < best_score - 1e-14:
                    best = p.copy()
                    best_score = best_sc
                    add_pool(pool, best, best_score)
                improved_any = True
                if verbose:
                    print(
                        "LP improvement", outer, inner,
                        f"{best_sc:.10f}",
                        "left", f"{max(0.0, deadline-time.monotonic()):.1f}s",
                        flush=True
                    )
                break

            if active.size < max_rows:
                add_count = max(1, min(1000, max_rows - active.size))
                add = np.argsort(b)[-add_count:]
                active = np.unique(np.concatenate((active, add)))
            else:
                break

        if not improved_any:
            break

    return best, best_score


def select_final_candidates(pool, count):
    """Choose a few low internal-score candidates from the pool."""
    if pool is None:
        return []
    available = list(range(len(pool)))
    chosen = []
    for _ in range(count):
        if not available:
            break
        best_idx = available[0]
        best_sc = float("inf")
        for idx in available:
            sc = float(pool[idx][0])
            if sc < best_sc:
                best_sc = sc
                best_idx = idx
        chosen.append(pool[best_idx][1])
        available.remove(best_idx)
    return chosen


def final_select(pool, internal_best, n, deadline):
    """Validate the best candidates with the external evaluator if available."""
    eval_func = globals().get("evaluate_sequence", None)
    best_seq = clean_sequence(internal_best, n)

    if not callable(eval_func):
        return [float(v) for v in best_seq]

    candidates = [internal_best]
    candidates.extend(select_final_candidates(pool, 9))

    best_exact = float("inf")
    for p in candidates:
        if time.monotonic() > deadline - 3.0:
            break
        seq = clean_sequence(p, n)
        try:
            exact = float(eval_func([float(v) for v in seq]))
        except Exception:
            exact = float("inf")
        if np.isfinite(exact) and exact < best_exact:
            best_exact = exact
            best_seq = seq

    return [float(v) for v in best_seq]


def propose_candidate(seed=42, budget_s=1800, **kwargs):
    """
    Main entry point.

    Uses a staged budget:
      1) cheap parametric/random initializer,
      2) FFT local search,
      3) cutting-plane LP refinement if helpful/time permits,
      4) another FFT local search pass,
      5) final exact validation among the best stored candidates.
    """
    start = time.monotonic()
    total = max(120.0, float(budget_s))
    deadline = start + max(0.0, total - 25.0)
    rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)

    n = choose_length()
    pool = []

    print(f"starting: n={n}, budget={budget_s:.0f}", flush=True)

    # Initial candidates: keep at most about 90 seconds, but never too little.
    cand_deadline = min(deadline - 700.0, start + 90.0)
    if cand_deadline < start + 5.0:
        cand_deadline = start + 5.0

    best, best_score = initial_candidates(n, rng, cand_deadline, pool)
    print(f"initial best internal={best_score:.10f}", flush=True)

    # First FFT local search phase.
    if time.monotonic() < deadline - 850.0:
        local_deadline = min(deadline - 850.0, start + 750.0)
        if local_deadline > start + 10.0:
            best, best_score = local_search(
                best, local_deadline, rng, n, pool, verbose=True
            )
            print(f"after local1 internal={best_score:.10f}", flush=True)

    # LP refinement is potentially expensive; use it if there is room.
    if _HAS_SCIPY and time.monotonic() < deadline - 450.0 and best_score > 1.47:
        lp_deadline = min(deadline - 300.0, start + int(total * 0.82))
        if lp_deadline > start + 60.0:
            best, best_score = active_lp_refine(
                best, lp_deadline, rng, n, pool, verbose=True
            )
            print(f"after LP internal={best_score:.10f}", flush=True)

    # Final FFT local polish.
    if time.monotonic() < deadline - 60.0:
        best, best_score = local_search(
            best, deadline - 45.0, rng, n, pool, verbose=False
        )
        print(f"after final local internal={best_score:.10f}", flush=True)

    seq = final_select(pool, best, n, deadline)
    print("done, returning sequence", flush=True)
    return seq
```
